[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Squid pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The Goblin Squid Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Goblin Squid Skin can be purchased from the Spring 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it costs 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Goblin Squid Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Squid Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

Tooltip: Health: +100; Gemstones: [] []; Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty (0\ 2); Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing (0\ 4); Grants +0 Trophy Chance.; Full Set Bonus: Star Fisher (0\ 4); Grants +0.7 Fishing Speed per ✪ Upgrade Star on your armor.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Expert Trophy Fisher.; * Soulbound * EPIC HELMET
Gold Hunter Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set used for [[Fishing]].
## Obtaining
Gold Hunter Armor can be obtained from [[Odger]] after reaching [[Odger|Expert Trophy Fisher]] rank. Once obtained, it can be purchased from Odger's [[Odger|Lost Rewards Shop]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] per piece.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Gold Hunter Helmet ([[Gold Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +100|Gold Hunter Chestplate ([[Gold Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +150|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Gold Hunter Leggings ([[Gold Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +125|Gold Hunter Boots ([[Gold Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +75|
[Total]
[Gold Hunter Armor Health: +450]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Star Fisher; Grants +0.7 Fishing Speed per ✪ Upgrade Star on your armor. Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing; Grants +0 (1 Piece)➜10 (2 Pieces)➜15 (3 Pieces)➜20 (4 Pieces) Trophy Chance. Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty; Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Due to its Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty, players no longer catch [[Sea Creatures]] and cannot do damage to them. However, they also gain immunity to any damage from Sea Creatures. The Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing also increases the player's chances of catching Trophy Fish.

## Tips
- Since Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty and Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing only require 2 pieces to activate, [[Froggles]] and a [[Red Sweater]] can be worn when fishing for [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Trivia
- If players would like to loot share Sea Creatures, they can get the damage requirement and switch to Trophy Hunter Armor in order to not die to them. This is especially noticeable for Boss [[Sea Creatures]], such as [[Thunder]] and [[Lord Jawbus]], since they do a lot of damage.
- Gold Hunter Armor nullifies the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]], without affecting the stat itself.
## History

|Mar 8, 2022|Added Gold Hunter Armor.|
|Nov 13, 2024|Added the Star Fisher Full Set Bonus.|
|Nov 13, 2024|Changed Odger's Blessing to grant +10[[Trophy Chance]] instead of a +10% chance to catch Trophy Fish.|
|Nov 25, 2024|Buffed Odger's Blessing from 10 to 20 [[Trophy Chance]]|
|Nov 25, 2024|The upgrade cost of Trophy Hunter armor has been standardized across all rarities.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Gold Hunter Armor.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Increased the [[Trophy Chance]] from 10 to 20.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|The upgrade cost of Trophy Hunter armor has been standardized across all rarities.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Gold Hunter Armor now grants 0.7 [[Fishing Speed]] per Star Upgrade ([[Star Upgrades]]) on every piece.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Odger's Blessing full set bonus changed to tiered bonus.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|[[Health]] changed from 625->345.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Removed [[Defense]]|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Combat Gemstone Slots ([[Gemstone Slot]]) changed to Aquamarine.|

Tooltip: Fishing Speed: +3; Ability: Reality Check Allows you to conceptualize and interact with Wormholes on the Lotus Atoll and Crimson Isle.; Ability: Hi-Vis Wormhole Helmet Allows you to get some vibes about far away Wormhole locations.; When fishing in Wormholes:; +5♔ Trophy Chance +5% chance to find GOLD Trophies.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 15.; EPIC HELMET
Golden Froggles are an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Lotus|Lotus VII (7)]]. They allow the player to view and fish in [[Wormhole|Wormholes]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lotus VII (7) ([[Lotus]])|1x [[Silver Froggles]] 2x [[Gold Lotus]] 64x [[Enchanted Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Gold Lotus]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] x32 ×2 + [[Silver Froggles]] → Golden Froggles|
## Usage
Helmet:
Golden Froggles [[Fishing Speed]]: +3
[Effects]
[Ability: Reality Check; Allows you to conceptualize and interact with Wormholes (Wormhole) on the Lotus Atoll and Crimson Isle. Ability: Hi-Vis Wormhole Helmet; Creates particles that point towards the nearest Wormhole.; When fishing in Wormholes (Wormhole): +5 Trophy Chance +5% chance to find GOLD Trophies]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Diamond Froggles]]|Lotus XI (11) ([[Lotus]])|[[Golden Froggles]] 1x Golden Froggles 2x [[Diamond Lotus]] 4x [[Condensed Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Diamond Lotus]] ×2 + [[Condensed Lily Pad]] x2 ×2 + Golden Froggles → [[Diamond Froggles]]|
Wearing Golden Froggles allows the player to interact with [[Wormhole|Wormholes]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] and [[Crimson Isle]]. Fishing in a [[Wormhole]] allows the player to catch [[Trophy Frogs|Reality Hopper]] [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Notes
- Golden Froggles, all of its predecessors, and its upgrade can uniquely have the [[Geometric Oddity]] reforge stone applied.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Retentio RIGHT CLICK Traps can be placed near the spawn location of a Shard creature, with time it will eventually catch it.; Ability: Forta Reduces the time to capture by 35%.; Sagacitas et fortitudo coniuncta.; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC TRAP
The Greater Huntrap is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Huntraps|Huntrap]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Tender Wood VII (7) ([[Tender Wood]])|1x [[Large Huntrap]] 64x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 256x [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x16 ×4 + [[Large Huntrap]] → Greater Huntrap|
## Usage
A Greater Huntrap can be placed near the spawn location of any creature that can drop a [[Attributes|Shard]] to collect one. The time to catch a shard is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught (see [[Huntraps|Huntraps#Time]]).
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Astral Huntrap]]|Tender Wood IX (9) ([[Tender Wood]])|[[Greater Huntrap]] 1x Greater Huntrap 4x [[Enchanted Tender Wood]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Tender Wood]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x32 ×4 + Greater Huntrap → [[Astral Huntrap]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntrap's description, "Sagacitas et fortitudo coniuncta." is Latin, and means "Acuteness and strength combined."

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +4 Farming Fortune for each of your Garden Levels.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Green Bandana is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It grants +4 [[Farming Fortune]] for each [[The Garden|Garden Level]] unlocked.
## Obtaining
### Garden Visitors
Green Bandana has a 0.4% (1/250) chance to be offered by [[Garden Visitors]] with [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher rarity in [[The Garden]], which means an overall chance of approximately 0.0878% (1/1,139) when considering visitors of all rarities.
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Green Bandana will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +4 [[Farming Fortune]] for each [[The Garden|Garden Level]] unlocked. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Green Bandana for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- Green Bandana can grant up to +60 [[Farming Fortune]], making it more effective than the [[Yellow Bandana]] (at Garden Level 8 or above), the [[Minos Relic]] and the [[Hephaestus Relic]] (on the [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]]).
## History

Type: Wand ([[Wands (disambiguation)]])

Collection ([[Collections]]): Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 12,288x (192 stacks (12,288 items total)) [[Null Sphere]] 491,520x (7,680 stacks (491,520 items total)) [[Obsidian]] 768,960x (12,015 stacks (768,960 items total)) [[Blaze Rod]] 9,600x (150 stacks (9,600 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GYROKINETIC_WAND
Tooltip: Ability: Gravity Storm LEFT CLICK Create a large rift at the aimed location, pulling all mobs together.; Regen mana 10x slower for 3s after cast.; Soulflow Cost: 10; Mana Cost: 1,200 Cooldown: 30s; Ability: Cells Alignment RIGHT CLICK Apply Aligned to yourself for 6s, plus 4 nearby players on grouped islands. (Catacombs, etc.); ⦀ Aligned Splits incoming damage and applies it over 3s.; Soulflow Cost: 2; Mana Cost: 220 Cooldown: 10s; ❣ Requires Enderman Slayer 6.; EPIC WAND
The Gyrokinetic Wand is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Wands|Wand]] obtained through [[Enderman Slayer]]. It requires [[Enderman|Enderman LVL VI (6)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|30x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] 30x [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] x16 ×6 + [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x30 + [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] x15 ×2 → Gyrokinetic Wand|
## Usage
Wand:
Gyrokinetic Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Gravity Storm LEFT CLICK; Create a large rift at the aimed location, pulling all mobs together.; Regen mana 10x slower for 3s after cast.; Soulflow Cost (Soulflow (Mechanic)): 10; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 1,200; Cooldown: 30s Ability: Cells Alignment RIGHT CLICK; Apply Aligned to yourself for 6s, plus 4 nearby players on grouped islands. (Catacombs, etc.); /// Aligned; Splits incoming damage and apply it over 3s.; Soulflow Cost (Soulflow (Mechanic)): 2; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 220; Cooldown: 10s]
When the Gravity Storm ability is used, the Gyrokinetic Wand creates a rift at the targeted location 10 blocks in radius. All mobs caught within the rift are pulled towards its center and held there for 4s. A wave of black and purple blocks appear: these are visual and have no collision. After the ability is used, its user's [[Mana]] regeneration is reduced by 10x for 3s. The ability costs 10 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] and 1200 [[Mana]] to use and has a cooldown of 30s. The ability can be used on every block from up to 24 blocks away from the player.
When the Cells Alignment ability is used, it applies the Aligned effect to its own user as well as up to 4 nearby players for 6s. When any player with Aligned takes damage, it is reduced by an amount, with the remaining damage applied over 3s instead. Whenever the player takes further damage, the delayed damage will refresh until either the player stops taking further damage or the buff expires. The ability costs 2 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] and 220 [[Mana]] to use and has a cooldown of 10s.
## Trivia
- Its left click ability has the highest Mana cost of any abilities with fixed mana cost other than [[Necromancy]] summons and abilities that consume a percentage or all your mana (i.e End Stone Bow)
- [[Ultimate Wise]] reduces the Mana costs of both abilities.
- The right click ability, Cells Alignment, is based off the League of Legends item Death's Dance, with it's passive component behaving identically to the item.
- The Aligned ability used to cause a bug that could kill Necromancy Summons.
- The Gravity Storm ability is commonly used in the [[Catacombs]], and [[The Mist]] to pull mobs together to prevent high damage attacks such as [[Fels]] and [[Shadow Assassin|Shadow Assassins]], and troll [[Ghost]] Farmers by pulling a lot of ghosts towards them. It can also be used in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] to pull the [[Thorn|Spirit Animals]] and [[Sadan|Terracottas]] together for killing them. In [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VI]], the community consider the Gyrokinetic Wand as a must have item.
- The Cells Alignment used to be bugged to reduce the damage from [[Kuudra|Dropships]] in the [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
- The split damage from Aligned is affected by your Defense, further reducing the damage dealt to you.

A Hashbrown is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Deep Fried]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Sunflower Oil]] 12x [[Deepfries]] 16x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: [[Deepfries]] x2 ×6 + [[Sunflower Oil]] x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x16 → Hashbrown|
## Usage
### Reforging
Hashbrown can be combined with a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Deep Fried]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]], which scales with the Farming Tool's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
Additionally, it grants the Deep Fried Bonus, which makes [[Seasoning|Seasonings]] 25% more common during [[Harvest Feast]] and [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] events.

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Sell: 240,000 [[Coins]]
Sell (Motes): 400 [[Motes]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2,400x (37 stacks plus 32 (2,400 in total)) [[Hemovibe]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HEMOBOMB
Tooltip: Collection Item; Consumable; Consume to gift 500 Motes to EVERYONE on your instance.; Cooldown: 2 hours; Right-click to consume!; X Rift-Transferable X EPIC
Hemobomb is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item and [[Crafting]] material. The recipe is unlocked at [[Hemovibe|Hemovibe IX (9)]], once the player has collected 100,000 (1,562 stacks plus 32) [[Hemovibe]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Hemovibe IX (9) ([[Hemovibe]])|15x [[Hemoglass]]|Recipe: [[Hemoglass]] x3 ×5 → Hemobomb|
## Usage
A Hemobomb can be consumed in the [[Rift Dimension]] to grant everyone in the player's current lobby 500 [[Motes]]. It has a cooldown of 2 hours between uses.
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming a Hemobomb|HEMOBOMB! [player] gave everyone 500 Motes!|
|Trying to consume a Hemobomb on cooldown|The hemobomb is on cooldown!|
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], a Hemobomb can be transferred to the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] through the Ender Chest behind the [[Dark Auction]]. Once in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], a Hemobomb can be used as described above.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blood Donor Artifact]]|Hemovibe IX (9) ([[Hemovibe]])|1x [[Blood Donor Ring]] [[Hemobomb]] 32x Hemobomb|Recipe: Hemobomb x4 ×8 + [[Blood Donor Ring]] → [[Blood Donor Artifact]]|

|[[Hephaestus Souvenir]]|None|[[Hephaestus Anvil]] 1x Hephaestus Anvil 3x [[Washed-up Souvenir]]|Recipe: [[Washed-up Souvenir]] ×3 + Hephaestus Anvil → [[Hephaestus Souvenir]]|

- The Hephaestus Anvil is named after Hephaestus, the god of blacksmithing in Greek Mythology.

The Hermit Baked Beans Ammonite Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 3, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,500 purchases.

The Hermit Graphite Ammonite Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 3, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Hermit Graphite Ammonite Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ammonite Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Hideonsack Shard

[Hideonsack Shard]

[Attribute]
Name: Sack Size ([[Attributes]])

Requirement: Hunting XV (15) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✕ No
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: HIDEONSACK
Tooltip: Sack Size I Your sacks can hold +1% more items.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E16)
The Hideonsack Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Sack Size]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Hideonsack Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Shulker Family
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hideonsack Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Sack Size]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Sack Size ([[Attributes]]); Your sacks can hold +1%–10% more items.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Hideonsack Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Hideonsack Shard has the following description:
- This shulker hides among storage areas.
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Hideonsack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Hideonsack.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Renamed Hideonsack to Hideonsack Shard.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Fixed [[Sacks]] not using the correct Sack size in combination with the Sack Size attribute (Hideonsack Shard).|

# Hocus-Pocus Cipher
Aliases: Hocus-Pocus Cipher (Rift), Hocus-pocus Cipher
Categories: Epic items, Accessories, Dark Auction
[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOCUS_POCUS_CIPHER
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; While in the rift:; +65 Rift Time +5 Intelligence +5 Mana Regen; X Rift-Transferable X Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Hocus-Pocus Cipher is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It can be [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Obtaining
The Hocus-Pocus Cipher has a chance of appearing in round 1, 3 or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]] with starting bid of 10,000,000 [[Coins]].
Hocus-Pocus Cipher can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Hocus-Pocus Cipher
[Effects]
[+65 Rift Time; +5 Intelligence; +5 Mana Regen]
## Bugs
- A Hocus-Pocus Cipher obtained from the [[Raffle of the Century]] cannot be transferred into the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] due to its custom NBT tag indicating it was obtained from a raffle. Players attempting to transfer it will receive the following error:
This item has been upgraded, enchanted, or modified! It cannot be transferred over to The Rift.
## Trivia
- Outside of the [[Rift Dimension]] it does not provide any stat boosts, same as [[Bingo Talisman]] and its upgrades, which means it can only grant boosts through [[Accessory Power]].
- A common misconception is that the term "Hocus Pocus" is of Czech origin, since the word "Pokus" means "trial" or "attempt" in Czech. This however is just a coincidence.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Hocus-Pocus Cipher.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Hocus-Pocus Cipher can now be enriched.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Hocus-Pocus Cipher.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Hocus-Pocus Cipher can now be enriched.|

The Hoe of Greatest Tilling is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] upgrade to Hoe of Greater Tilling. Instead of its predecessors, this hoe tills entire row, regardless of blocks affected.
## Obtaining
Hoe of Greatest Tilling can be purchased from [[Anita]] for 2 [[Medals|Silver Medal]] and 20 [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].

Hoe:

[Tills a row of farmland. The perfect accessory to the Basket of Seeds!]
## Trivia
- [[Grass|Grass Blocks]], [[Dirt]], [[Coarse Dirt]] and [[Podzol]] are all affected by the Hoe of Greatest Tilling's effect.
- Despite not being crafted from a lower tier item, this is the upgraded form of [[Hoe of Greater Tilling]].

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Bee pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The Honey Bee Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Honey Bee Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 24, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Honey Bee Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Bee Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
## Gallery
- Honey Bee Skin
## History

|Feb 22, 2024|Announced the [[Fire Sale]] for the Honey Bee Skin.|
|Feb 24, 2024|Started the [[Fire Sale]] for the Honey Bee Skin.|

A Horn of Taurus is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item dropped by [[Taurus]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv262]|[[Taurus]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Horns of Torment]]|Raw Mutton IX (9) ([[Raw Mutton]])|[[Horn of Taurus]] 32x Horn of Taurus 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x24 ×8 + Horn of Taurus x32 → [[Horns of Torment]]|
|[[Lava Water Orb]]|None|1x [[Water Orb]] 2x [[Thunder Fragment]] [[Horn of Taurus]] 16x Horn of Taurus 32x [[Silver Magmafish]] 32x [[Flaming Heart]] 32x [[Cup of Blood]] 64x [[Lump of Magma]] 64x [[Moogma Pelt]]|Recipe: [[Silver Magmafish]] x16 ×2 + [[Thunder Fragment]] x2 + [[Moogma Pelt]] x64 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Lump of Magma]] x64 + [[Cup of Blood]] x32 + Horn of Taurus x16 + [[Flaming Heart]] x32 → [[Lava Water Orb]]|

Horns of Taurus are used to add [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] to pieces of gear:

- [[Hellfire Rod]]
- [[Thunder Armor]]

# Horns of Torment
Aliases: Demonic, Horns Of Torment
Categories: Epic items, Power stones, Craftable items, Raw Mutton Collection, Power Stones
[Horns of Torment]
Type: Accessory Power Stone
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Raw Mutton IX (9) ([[Raw Mutton]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Power: Demonic
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 32x [[Horn of Taurus]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Raw Mutton]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HORNS_OF_TORMENT
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Demonic power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +178.36&#xE00D; Strength +895.69&#xE003; Intelligence; Unique Power Bonus:; +50&#xE007; Crit Damage; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 25.; EPIC POWER STONE
The Horns of Torment is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Demonic]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Strength]] and [[Intelligence]]. It also provides +50 [[Crit Damage]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Mutton IX (9) ([[Raw Mutton]])|32x [[Horn of Taurus]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x24 ×8 + [[Horn of Taurus]] x32 → Horns of Torment|
## Requirements
The Horns of Torment requires [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Horns of Torment at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Demonic [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]]'s Fusion-Style Potato perk doubles the effects of Hot Potato Books.
## Trivia
- Players used to be able to craft Hot Potato Books by buying the resources from the [[Farm Merchant]] for 59,748.3333 [[Coins]]. This method is no longer feasible as players can only buy up to 640 (10 stacks) [[Potato|Potatoes]] a day.

# Hurricane Bow
Categories: Epic items, Weapons, Craftable items, Bone Collection, Bows

[Item Properties]

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 15,360x (240 stacks (15,360 items total)) [[Bone]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[String]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HURRICANE_BOW
Tooltip: Damage: +120; Strength: +50; Ability: Tempest The more kills you get using this bow the more powerful it becomes! Reach 250 kills to unlock its full potential.; Next Upgrade: Double Shot (0\ 20); Kills: 0; This item can be reforged!; EPIC BOW
The Hurricane Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] unlocked at [[Bone|Bone VII (7)]]. Its ability allows it to shoot up to 5 arrows at once depending on the amount of kills on the bow, capping at 250 kills for 5 arrows at once.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Bone VII (7) ([[Bone]])|96x [[Enchanted Bone]] 96x [[String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Bone]] x32 ×3 + [[String]] x32 ×3 → Hurricane Bow|
## Usage
Bow:
Hurricane Bow [[Damage]]: +120 [[Strength]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Tempest; The more kills you get using this bow the more powerful it becomes! Reach 250 kills to unlock its full potential]
The Hurricane Bow's item ability increases the number of [[Arrows]] (up to 5 arrows) shot together by killing more entities (passive or hostile) and can be maxed out at 250 kills. The additional arrows come out at a 7.5° and 15° offset from the main arrow. Unlike the [[Runaan's Bow]], the extra arrows deal 100% of the main arrow's damage.
## Bugs
- Since the NBT tag of the Hurricane Bow updates after every kill, it will cancel the draw animation if an arrow kills a mob while drawing a new shot.
## Trivia
- For a few hours after update v0.7.2, all 5 arrows of the Hurricane Bow were affected by the Aiming enchantment, making it more effective than [[Runaan's Bow]]. This was found to be a bug and was quickly removed.
- This bow has sometimes been used to fight [[Bal]] due to each attack with the maxed bow does 5 damage to it.
- Without any [[Attack Speed]], this bow is very powerful against mobs that requires hits, since it can fire 5 arrows with 250 kills, and 7 arrows with maxed [[Terror Armor]] stacks.
  - It is especially significant on [[Reindrake]], because of it being a dragon, which [[Dragon Tracer]] can deal hits on it without missing a lot, and it takes hits to get loot and kill.
- The Hurricane, along with [[Runaan's Bow]], are references to Runaan's Hurricane, a bow in League of Legends that fires multiple arrows.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Aug 15, 2019|Fixed Hurricane Bow displaying the wrong amount of arrows whenever it gets upgraded.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|A bug made all 5 arrows of the Hurricane Bow affected by Aiming ([[Dragon Tracer]]).|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Fixed the aforementioned bug.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Hurricane Bow keeping its sell price lore when exiting a shop.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Hurricane Bow as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Hyper Catalyst
Categories: Epic items, Minion fuels, Craftable items, Minion Modifiers
[Hyper Catalyst]
Type: [[Minion Fuel]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Catalyst]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[End Stone]] 8x [[Crystal Fragment]] 1x [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HYPER_CATALYST
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; When in a minion, this increases the number of resources created by 4x for 6 hours!; EPIC
The Hyper Catalyst is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Fuel]] that multiplies minion resource generation by 4x for 6 hours.
It quadruples the resources the minions would normally get from harvesting a block or a mob, however, like its predecessor, the [[Catalyst]], it has no effect in cases such as a mob grinder which kills the mobs automatically. Hyper Catalyst gives the second highest increase in production efficiency of all [[Minion Fuel|Minion Fuels]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]] 8x [[Catalyst]]|Recipe: [[Catalyst]] ×8 + [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]] → Hyper Catalyst x8|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Hyper Catalysts increase the amount of resources generated by the minion by 4x for 6 hours. Hyper Catalyst can be placed in all [[Minions]].
## Tips
- Despite Hyper Catalyst giving the second highest increase in production efficiency, for most money making minion setups it is not profitable to use due to the cost. [[Tasty Cheese]] is a cheaper alternative that is profitable more often, but can still be less profitable than an infinite duration fuel (e.g. [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] and [[Plasma Bucket]]) for some setups.
- A minion must generate at least per day to be more profitable when using Hyper Catalysts.
## Trivia
- Previously, this item was bugged, giving only 1.5x the materials collected from minions that harvest multiple items per action (Such as [[Cocoa Beans Minion|Cocoa Beans Minions]]).
- Spreading upgrades (e.g. [[Diamond Spreading]] and [[Sleepy Hollow]]) will apply to the extra drops gained from Hyper Catalyst's bonus.
- The [[Minion Fuel]] that gives a higher increase in production efficiency than Hyper Catalyst is [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], which can only be applied to [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]]. So for a general minion, Hyper Catalyst gives the highest increase in production efficiency.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Hyper Catalyst.|

The Hyper Cleaver is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
A Hyper Cleaver can be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 800,000 [[Coins]] and [[Super Cleaver]] upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor III]].
## Usage
Sword:
Hyper Cleaver [[Damage]]: +175 [[Strength]]: +100 [[Crit Damage]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Cleave; When hitting an entity, monsters in a 4 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.]
The Hyper Cleaver, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Cleave ability causes mobs within a radius of 4 blocks to take a portion of damage dealt to the wielder's target.
### Merchants
A Hyper Cleaver alongside 5,000,000 [[Coins]] is required to purchase [[Giant Cleaver]] from [[Ophelia]].
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|5|5|
|✪✪|10|15|
|✪✪✪|15|30|
|✪✪✪✪|20; 10,000 [[Coins]]|50; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|25; 25,000 [[Coins]]|75; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Hyper Cleaver, due to its high stats, comfortably outclasses other weapons such as a [[Aspect of the Dragons]] or [[Livid Dagger]], and is highly recommended as a primary weapon.
## Trivia
- The item ability item effect#Cleave Cleave from League of Legends shares a name as and possesses a similar effect to the Hyper Cleaver.
- Despite its built-in ability, this weapon can still be given the [[Cleave]] enchantment.

## Obtaining
The Iceberg Skin could be purchased from the Winter 2023 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.

## Obtaining
### Fusion
An Iguana Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Gecko Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Forest Category

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Iguana Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Atomized]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Atomized ([[Attributes]]); Your Atomized Attributes are +2%–20% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Inferno Vertex (plural: vertices) is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is created once in a while by [[Inferno Minion]] with [[Hypergolic Gabagool]].
## Obtaining
### Inferno Minion
Inferno Vertex has a 0.0168% (1/5,950) chance to be generated per harvest from an [[Inferno Minion]] using [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|6x [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x16 ×4 + [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]] x6 → Inferno Vertex|

# InfiniBoom™ TNT
Aliases: Infinityboom TNT
Categories: Epic items, Items, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools, Dungeon Items

Buy: 50,000 (781 stacks plus 16) [[Undead Essence]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INFINITE_SUPERBOOM_TNT
Tooltip: Blows up cracked brick walls and crypts, which are typically found in Dungeons and the Crystal Hollows.; Cooldown: 2s; EPIC
InfiniBoom™ TNT is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that acts like [[Superboom TNT]]. However, unlike [[Superboom TNT]], InfiniBoom™ TNT can be reused over and over with a 2-second cooldown in between placements.
## Obtaining
InfiniBoom™ TNT can be obtained from the [[Essence Shops|Undead Essence Shop]] from [[Malik]] for 50,000 [[Undead Essence]].
## Usage
Item:
InfiniBoom™ TNT
[Effects]
[Blows up cracked brick walls and crypts, which are typically found in The Catacombs (Catacombs) and the Crystal Hollows.; Cooldown: 2s]
## Tips
- It is recommended to save the [[Essence|Undead Essence]] for something else, as [[Superboom TNT]] is relatively cheap.
  - Alternatively, class abilities or a [[Conjuring]] can be used as a replacement to InfiniBoom™ TNT.
## History

The InfiniLeap™ is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that acts like a [[Spirit Leap]]. However, unlike [[Spirit Leap]], an [[InfiniLeap™]] InfiniLeap™ can be reused over and over with a 2-second cooldown.
## Obtaining
The InfiniLeap™ can be obtained from the [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]] from [[Malik]] for 25,000 [[Wither Essence]].
InfiniLeap™ can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Item:
InfiniLeap™
[Effects]
[Ability: Spirit Leap RIGHT CLICK; Allows you to teleport to any teammate! Grants 1 second of immunity after teleporting, immunity is cancelled upon dealing damage.; Cooldown: 2s]
Compared to the [[Spirit Leap]], the Infinileap can be used infinitely, and has a 2 second cooldown instead of 5 seconds.
## Tips
- It is recommended to save the [[Wither Essence]] for something else, as a [[Spirit Leap]] is relatively inexpensive.

# InfiniTorch™
Aliases: InfiniTorch
Categories: Epic items, Items
[InfiniTorch™]
Type: Utility
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Lumina]]
Buy: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INFINI_TORCH
Tooltip: Places a torch for 2 coins.; Right-Click a block to use!; EPIC
The InfiniTorch™ is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can place torches for 2 [[Coins]] on the player's [[Private Island]] or in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Its ability is similar to that of the [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]].
## Obtaining
The InfiniTorch™ can be purchased from [[Lumina]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Item:
InfiniTorch™
[Effects]
[Places a torch for 2 coins.]
## Trivia
- Its name is a reference to the [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]], which also places an item for [[Coins]].
- There are more cost-effective ways to get torches, including buying them from [[Lumina]], the [[Mining Merchant|Mine Merchant]], or the [[Builder]]; or buying a [[Lantern]] from [[Lumina]] and coal for it from the [[Bazaar]] or another merchant. However, you may consider buying an InfiniTorch for convenience, as the Lantern requires you to have coal in your inventory and it cannot be in enchanted or block form.
- Unlike the [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]], You can not put it in the [[Builder's Wand]] or the [[Builder's Ruler]].

The InfiniVacuum™ is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Vacuums|Vacuum]].
## Obtaining
The InfiniVacuum™ can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[SkyMart Hyper Vacuum]] and 1,000 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Vacuum:
InfiniVacuum™ [[Damage]]: +300
[Effects]
[Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Deals 300 Damage per second to nearby Pests (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK; Leads you in the direction of the nearest Pest (Pest (Mob Type)).; Cooldown: 1s]
The InfiniVacuum™ can be used to capture [[Pests]]. When used near a pest, it will deal 300 [[Damage]] per second. [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] captured with the SkyMart Hyper Vacuum will be sucked up, and can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]] and various goods at his shop.
Left Clicking with the InfiniVacuum™ will create a trail of particles leading to the nearest Pest.

The Ink Wand is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Ink Sac|Ink Sac IX (9)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting

Sword:
Ink Wand [[Damage]]: +130 [[Strength]]: +90
[Effects]
[Ability: Ink Bomb RIGHT CLICK; Shoot an ink bomb in front of you dealing; 10,000 damage and giving Blindness (Blindness Potion)!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 60; Cooldown: 30s]
The Ink Wand's Ink Bomb ability fires an Ink Sac covered in black particles in an arc. The Ink Sac deals 10,000 damage to all mobs in a 0.5 block radius. It costs 60 [[Mana]] and has a 30s cooldown.
The [[Damage]] and [[Strength]] of the Ink Wand can be increased with a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Squid Pet]] or higher, up to +30 [[Damage]] and +10 [[Strength]] at [[Rarity|RARE]] and +40 [[Damage]] and +20 [[Strength]] at [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].

Isopod Husk is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item used to increase the player's max [[Respiration]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv186]|[[Giant Isopod]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
## Usage
Up to 5 Isopod Husks can be consumed to permanently gain +1 [[Respiration]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|2 SkyBlock XP]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Husk|CRUNCH! You try to chew, but it's tougher than a jawbreaker.|
|Second Husk|TOUGH! How do the isopod's predators get through this stuff? What even hunts something that big?|
|Third Husk|FLOSS! You can feel all the fragments of exoskeleton stuck between your teeth.|
|Fourth Husk|BLEH! It really doesn't get any easier.|
|Fifth Husk|DONE! Not a fun experience, wouldn't recommend.|
|Attempting to consume more than 5|You've already consumed the maximum amount of this item!|
## History

|Jul 22, 2026|Changed Isopod Husk texture.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Isopod Husk.|

The Ivory Black Cat Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Ivory Black Cat Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 27, 2021. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 10,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Ivory Black Cat Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Black Cat Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JADERALD
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Jaded reforge when combined with Armor.; Extremely rare stone, only found in the Crystal Hollows.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Jaderald is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Jaded]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
A Jaderald has a 0.465920% (1 in 214.6) chance to be rolled from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] Treasure Stash after all five [[Gemstone Crystals]] are placed.
## Usage
### Reforging
Jaderald can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Jaded]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Jaded]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

- Its name is a portmanteau of the words Jade and Emerald.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JALAPENO_BOOK
Tooltip: Combine this Book in an Anvil with a deployable to gain a small but permanent stat boost!; EPIC
A Jalapeno Book is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be applied to [[Deployables]] to upgrade them.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Chili Pepper VII (7) ([[Chili Pepper]])|1x [[Stuffed Chili Pepper]] 3x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] ×3 + [[Stuffed Chili Pepper]] → Jalapeno Book|
## Usage
A Jalapeno Book can be applied once to a [[Deployables|Deployable]] in an [[Anvil]]. While the [[Deployables|Deployable]] is active, it grants an additional +5 [[Crit Damage]] and +1 [[Crit Chance]] in addition to the buffs it usually provides.
## Trivia
- Despite the item being a reference to jalapeños, the item's name spells it as 'jalapeno'.
- Applying a Jalapeno Book to a [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Hole]] does not grant stats when deployed.

## Usage
Drill:
Jasper Drill X [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +9 [[Mining Speed]]: +600 [[Pristine]]: +2 [[Gemstone Fortune]]: +120
[Effects]
[Grants +20% ᠅ Gemstone Powder, and +800 Mining Speed when mining Gemstones.]
The Jasper Drill X has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Jasper Drill X's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
This drill also gives a 20% Extra ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] which makes it good for powder mining.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JERRY_STAFF
Tooltip: Damage: +80; Intelligence: +200; Ability: Rapid-fire RIGHT CLICK Shoots a Jerry bullet, dealing 500; damage on impact and knocking you back.; Each shot costs +30 mana more than the previous, resetting after 4s of not firing.; This item can be reforged!; EPIC SWORD
The Jerry-chine Gun is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Weapons|Weapon]] obtainable from [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]] while [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]] is active. It fires Jerry bullets at a high rate.
## Obtaining
The Jerry-chine Gun can be obtained from any [[Jerry Box]].
## Usage
Sword:
Jerry-chine Gun [[Damage]]: +80 [[Intelligence]]: +200
[Effects]
[Ability: Rapid-fire RIGHT CLICK; Shoots a Jerry bullet, dealing 500 damage on impact and knocking you back.; Each shot costs +30 mana more than the previous, resetting after 4s of not firing.; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
When used, it shoots bullets resembling Jerry heads in the direction the player is facing. It has a maximum fire rate of 5 per second[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and it has a 3° angle of spread. The bullets are destroyed on impact, dealing 500+ [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] to enemies which are directly hit.
If the user is within 2.5 blocks of the impact, they are knocked upwards 2.5 blocks and away in the flat plane up to 2.5 blocks. Shooting other players with the bullets has no effect.
The ability costs 30 [[Mana]] to use, and increases by 30 [[Mana]] each shot. If 4 seconds pass without shooting, its [[Intelligence|Mana Cost]] resets to 30.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|5 Essence|5 Essence|
|✪✪|10 Essence|15 Essence|
|✪✪✪|25 Essence|40 Essence|
|✪✪✪✪|50 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|90 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|100 Essence; 25,000 [[Coins]]|190 Essence; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Jerry-chine Gun can shoot through 1-block thick walls.
- The Jerry-chine Gun has the highest fire rate of any weapon in the game.[Confirm]
- The user can scale flat surfaces and medium-sized walls with it by looking down and shooting at the ground. This property of the weapon can be used in the [[Maxor]] & [[Goldor]] boss fights in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], by scaling the walls to reach the crystals in the first phase and the second device in the third phase respectively.
- Starring the Jerry-chine Gun makes it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
- Before the ability Gold's Power of the [[Golden Dragon Pet]] was removed, it affected the Jerry-chine Gun.

## Obtaining
The Jinn Goblin Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on May 31, 2025. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 4,000 purchases.

The Jinn Goblin Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Goblin Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

[Upgrades]

Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOODOO_DOLL_WILTED
Tooltip: Ability: Acupuncture RIGHT CLICK Shoot arrows from every direction around the targeted monster.; Monsters hit by at least one arrow are slowed and receive 2,222 damage\ s for 12s.; Mana Cost: 180 Cooldown: 3s; Who do you voodoo?; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 5.; EPIC
The Jinxed Voodoo Doll is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is unlocked at [[Wilted Berberis|Wilted Berberis III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wilted Berberis III (3) ([[Wilted Berberis]])|1x [[Voodoo Doll]] 1x Voidling Minion II ([[Voidling Minion]]) 1x [[Zombie's Heart]] 3x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 13x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] 64x [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]]|Recipe: [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x3 + [[Voidling Minion]] + [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x13 + [[Zombie's Heart]] + [[Voodoo Doll]] → Jinxed Voodoo Doll|
## Usage
Item:
Jinxed Voodoo Doll
[Effects]
[Ability: Acupuncture RIGHT CLICK; Shoot arrows from every direction around the targeted monster.; Monsters hit by at least one arrow are slowed and receive 2,222 damage/s for 12s.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 180; Cooldown: 3; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 1]
The Jinxed Voodoo Doll's Acupuncture ability can be activated by aiming at any entity within a range of 18 blocks. Activating the ability summons a volley of 9 arrows around the targeted mobs: 4 in the cardinal horizontal directions, 4 aimed 45˚ downwards in the cardinal diagonal directions, and 1 above the mob that travels downward.
All mobs that are hit by the volley of arrows take their base damage, as well as an additional 2,222 base damage as damage per second over 12s. The ability costs 180 [[Mana]] to use and has a 3s cooldown.
## Trivia
- Attempting to feed [[Siamese Lynxes]] a Jinxed Voodoo Doll will result in the message This mob doesn't eat voodoo.
- The Jinxed Voodoo Doll can target player-summoned undead mobs, [[Berserk]] ultimate ability minions, [[Wither Armor]] Witherborns, and bats from the [[Spirit Sceptre]].
- The Jinxed Voodoo Doll's description "Who do you voodoo?" is a reference to the video game Dead Island, where at the start of the game, the song "Who Do You Voodoo" will play.
- The cooldown on both voodoo dolls is shared, for example if a player uses Voodoo Doll they will have a 5 second cooldown on Jinxed Voodoo Doll and vice versa.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Jinxed Voodoo Doll can now be donated to the [[Museum]].|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Fixed Jinxed Voodoo Doll and [[Voodoo Doll]] not sharing their ability cooldown.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JUJU_SHORTBOW
Tooltip: Damage: +310; Strength: +40; Crit Chance: +10%; Crit Damage: +110%; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s; Hits 3 mobs on impact.; Can damage endermen.; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 5.; EPIC BOW
The Juju Shortbow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|32x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] 32x [[Null Ovoid]] 32x [[Enchanted Quartz Block]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x32 + [[Enchanted String]] x64 ×3 + [[Null Ovoid]] x32 + [[Enchanted Quartz Block]] x32 → Juju Shortbow|
## Usage
Bow:
Juju Shortbow [[Damage]]: +310 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Crit Chance]]: +10 [[Crit Damage]]: +110
[Effects]
[Hits 3 mobs on impact.; Can damage endermen.]
The Juju Shortbow, upon impact, creates a 3-block radius that damages up to 3 mobs, with each mob taking the full initial damage. The Juju Shortbow can damage [[Enderman|Endermen]].
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded with Dragon Essence. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XV (15)]] to use.
Essence Required; [[Dragon Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|150|150|
|✪|30|180|
|✪✪|60|240|
|✪✪✪|120|360|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|560; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|860; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Fire rate
When left-clicking, the fire rate starts at 2.22 shots per second (9 ticks per shot) with 0 [[Attack Speed]] and increases to 5 shots per second (4 ticks per shot) with 100 [[Attack Speed]]. The increase in fire rate with [[Attack Speed]] is incremental, not continuous. The maximum possible fire rate that can be achieved by optimal left-clicking depending on the player's [[Attack Speed]]:
|Shot Cooldown|[[Attack Speed]] Lowest|[[Attack Speed]] Highest|Shots per second|
|-|-|-|-|
|0.45s(9 ticks)|< -8|11|2.22|
|0.35s(7 ticks)|12|40|2.86|
|0.25s(5 ticks)|41|66|4.00|
|0.20s(4 ticks)|67|100|5.00|
## Trivia
- It is one of 5 bows which can damage Endermen, the others being the [[Explosive Bow]], [[Spider Queen's Stinger]], [[Terminator]], and [[Bonemerang]].
- If Arrows are in the Inventory when right-clicking, the bow drawback animation will play, causing the player to slow down, and arrows are not fired at the normal rate. Left-clicking still works as intended.
- Before the Trading Update, you could use this weapon inside dungeons without having Enderman Slayer 3 if you had the Catacombs requirement to use it. However, this glitch was patched.
- The lore implies that the AOE ability deals damage on the spot of impact. However, it used to take effect around the player instead of the mob the arrows hit. This is a patched bug.
- When left-clicking on a mob with the Juju Shortbow in melee range, arrows will not be fired. Right-clicking will fire arrows as intended.
- This, along with the Terminator, were the first shortbows.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JUNGLE_KEY
Tooltip: Used to open the entrance to the Jungle Temple.; EPIC
The Jungle Key is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to open the [[Jungle Temple]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv120]|[[Key Guardian]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Shop Purchase
A Jungle Key can be purchased from [[Odawa]] for 100 [[Sludge Juice]].
### Garden Visitor
A Jungle Key can be obtained as a reward from [[Odawa]] when they visit [[The Garden]].
## Usage
A Jungle Key can be given to either [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]]. In return, the door to the [[Jungle Temple]] will open, as well as the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] and 2 [[Chest|Chests]] at the end of the parkour made available.
## Trivia
- It is possible to enter the [[Jungle Temple]] without a Jungle Key by breaking specific blocks, but the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] cannot be claimed unless a Jungle Key has been used.
  - However, the 2 [[Chest|Chests]] at the end of the parkour can be looted even without an active Jungle Key.

The Kindlebane Dagger is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Firedust Dagger]] 1x [[Gold Magmafish]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Gold Magmafish]] + [[Molten Powder]] x8 + [[Firedust Dagger]] → Kindlebane Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Kindlebane Dagger [[Damage]]: +130 [[Strength]]: +55 [[Crit Damage]]: +20 [[Attack Speed]]: +10
[Effects]
[Deal 1.5x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 1.2x damage to Wither mobs.; Gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Ashen and Auric mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Burning Vengeance ﬗ; On hit, repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Kindlebane Dagger deals 1.5x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 1.2x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +30 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Kindlebane Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Ashen and Auric attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Kindlebane Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Burning Vengeance allows the wielder to repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement. This does not affect the damage immunity of demons, and has a cap of 25,000,000 [[Damage]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pyrochaos Dagger]]|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Kindlebane Dagger]] 1x Kindlebane Dagger 8x [[Gold Magmafish]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Gold Magmafish]] x8 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + Kindlebane Dagger → [[Pyrochaos Dagger]]|

### Hunting
A King Minos Shard can be obtained from killing [[King Minos]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the King Minos Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|King of Greed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- King of Greed ([[Attributes]]); Increases Coin ([[Coins]]) gain from [[Griffin Burrows]] by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KRAMPUS_HELMET
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; Gains +1 per 50 gifts given by wearer.; Max 500; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Place it in ONE minion and it will occasionally generate Red Gifts!; This item can be reforged!; EPIC HELMET
Krampus Helmet is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] that gives +1 for every 50 [[Gifts]] given by the wearer, capping at 500 [[Health]] (25,000 Gifts). It also works as a [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]], giving a Minion a 0.0045% chance to generate a [[Red Gift]] per item generated.
## Obtaining
The Krampus Helmet has a 0.10% (1 in 969.12 gifts) chance to drop from opening [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]].
## Usage
### Armor
Helmet:
Krampus Helmet
[Effects]
[Gains +1 Health per 50 Gifts given by wearer.; Maximum of 500 Health .]
### Minion Upgrade
Krampus Helmet can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It is limited to one minion per profile at a time. For every item that the minion harvests, it has a 0.000045 (1 in 22222.22) chance to generate a [[Red Gift]]. Minions that collect multiple items at once, such as [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]], can make multiple red gifts at once. Krampus Helmet does not affect the minion's speed or other resources generated by the minion.
## Trivia
- The item was auctioned by former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon before its official release.
- The [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]] result in per item generated by the minion.
- In Central European mythology, Krampus is an evil demon who punishes naughty kids (as opposed to Saint Nicholas, who rewards good kids).
- Placing a Krampus Helmet in a minion and then picking up the Minion removes all enchantments and reforges. However, the helmet can be safely taken out using the minion's UI.
## Gallery
- The Krampus Helmet first seen on Minikloon's auction.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 24, 2019|First seen on Minikloon's auction.|
|Dec 24, 2019|Added Krampus Helmet.|
|Apr 15, 2021|Leaked minion upgrade usage by Minikloon.|
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Krampus Helmet is now a Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]]) which occasionally produces Red Gifts ([[Red Gift]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Krampus Helmet can now be salvaged.|

# Kuudra Follower Artifact
Aliases: Kuudra Artifact, Kuudra artifact
Categories: Epic items, Accessories

[Upgrades]

Next →: [[Kuudra Follower Relic]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Reduces damage taken from Kuudra Followers by 50%.; * Co-op Soulbound * EPIC ACCESSORY
The Kuudra Follower Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that halves damage taken from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Followers]].
## Obtaining
The Kuudra Follower Artifact can be purchased from [[Drakuu]] at [[The Wasteland]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The player must be wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], and there must be a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] eruption taking place.
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|[[Drakuu]]|100,000,000 [[Coins]] 1,024x (16 stacks (1,024 items total)) [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 1,024x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 64x [[Kuudra Teeth]]|
## Usage
Accessory:
Kuudra Follower Artifact
[Effects]
[Reduces damage taken from Kuudra Followers (Kuudra Follower) by 50%.]
### Upgrading
The Kuudra Follower Artifact can be upgraded into the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] by speaking to [[Crag]] in the [[Forgotten Skull]] while wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], and while having the [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]].
## Trivia
- Prior to 0.18.3, this was the best source of Damage Reduction overall.
- Prior to 0.17.2, to purchase the item, it required all players in the same lobby being in the air via either floating in midair or standing on the very edge of a block.

|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Description reworded.|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

# Kuudra Teeth
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Kuudra
[Kuudra Teeth]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Medium Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Large Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_TEETH
Tooltip: Also known as mandibles.; EPIC
Kuudra Teeth are [[Rarity|EPIC]] items used to prestige [[Armor]] sets obtained from Kuudra.
## Obtaining
### Kuudra
Kuudra Teeth have a 100% chance to appear in a Paid Reward Chest from [[Kuudra]].
Tier: Amount
Basic: 1
Hot: 1
Burning: 2
Fiery: 2
Infernal: 3
### Kuudra Boss Collection
On top of getting them from Paid Reward Chests, players may also receive them from Kuudra's boss collection, as seen below.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|10|Common Kuudra Pet ([[Kuudra Pet]]) Reward; 400 (6 stacks plus 16) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] Kuudra Teeth Reward|+10|
|II|100|[[Kuudra Chunk]] Reward; 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] 2 Kuudra Teeth Reward|+15|
|III|500|Meaty Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 2,500 (39 stacks plus 4) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] 4 Kuudra Teeth Reward|+20|
|IV|2,000|Hardened Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 10,000 (156 stacks plus 16) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] 8 Kuudra Teeth Reward|+25|
|V|5,000|Enriched Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 20,000 (312 stacks plus 32) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] 12 Kuudra Teeth Reward|+30|
## Usage
### Upgrading Armor
Kuudra Teeth are used in Essence Crafting to prestige certain Armor sets:
- [[Aurora Armor]]
- [[Crimson Armor]]
- [[Fervor Armor]]
- [[Hollow Armor]]
- [[Terror Armor]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Tabasco II ([[Tabasco]]))|Chili Pepper IX (9) ([[Chili Pepper]])|[[Kuudra Teeth]] 3x Kuudra Teeth 32x [[Chili Pepper]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] x16 ×2 + Kuudra Teeth ×3 → [[Tabasco]]|
### Shop Purchase
A total of [[Kuudra Teeth]] 64 (1 stack) Kuudra Teeth, 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Red Sand]], 1,024 [[Enchanted Mycelium]], and 100,000,000 [[Coins]] are required to purchase the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] from [[Drakuu]]. This shop is accessed by wearing a full set of [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] during a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] eruption.
## History

|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Kuudra Teeth are no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Kuudra Teeth as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

L.A.S.R.'s Eye is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Precursor Relic]]. It can be used to upgrade [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] into the [[Hyperion]], and is also used to upgrade [[Wither Armor]] into [[Storm's Armor]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
L.A.S.R.'s Eye has a 50% chance to be dropped from [[L.A.S.R.]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] (Summoned by [[The Watcher]]).
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv485-895]|[[L.A.S.R. (Watcher)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hyperion]]|None|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] 8x L.A.S.R.'s Eye One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: L.A.S.R.'s Eye ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Hyperion]]|
|Storm's Helmet ([[Storm's Armor]])|None|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] 8x L.A.S.R.'s Eye Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: L.A.S.R.'s Eye ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Storm's Armor]]|
|Storm's Chestplate ([[Storm's Armor]])|None|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] 8x L.A.S.R.'s Eye Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: L.A.S.R.'s Eye ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Storm's Armor]]|
|Storm's Leggings ([[Storm's Armor]])|None|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] 8x L.A.S.R.'s Eye Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: L.A.S.R.'s Eye ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Storm's Armor]]|
|Storm's Boots ([[Storm's Armor]])|None|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] 8x L.A.S.R.'s Eye Wither Boots ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: L.A.S.R.'s Eye ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Storm's Armor]]|
## Trivia
- The lore of this item may come from the sentence "The pen is mightier than the sword", which is from the play Richelieu; Or the Conspiracy by Edward Bulwer-Lytton.
- When doing Floor 7 frag runs, this is the most profitable relic.

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8x [[Bottled Mischief]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Ruby Veilshroom]] 128,000x (2,000 stacks (128,000 items total)) [[Helix Log]] 10,240x (160 stacks (10,240 items total)) [[Mangrove Log]] 10,240x [[Obsidian]] 64x [[Fig Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LARGE_POCKET_BLACK_HOLE
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Black Hole RIGHT CLICK Place the Black Hole for 180s, and it'll start pulling low HP monsters towards it.; Cooldown: 2s; Harness the power of Gravity... and Mushrooms!; Monsters below 10% HP can even get their Shard captured!; Ability: Blessing from the Void Grants 5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this Black Hole!; EPIC DEPLOYABLE
A Large Pocket Black Hole is a [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Deployables|Deployable]]. It is unlocked at [[Ruby Veilshroom|Ruby Veilshroom VIII (8)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ruby Veilshroom VIII (8) ([[Ruby Veilshroom]])|1x [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]] 2x [[Veilshroom Bunch]] 5x [[Helixis]] 8x [[Bottled Mischief]]|Recipe: [[Veilshroom Bunch]] ×2 + [[Bottled Mischief]] x8 + [[Helixis]] ×5 + [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]] → Large Pocket Black Hole|
## Usage
A Large Pocket Black Hole can be placed to pull in mobs under 10% [[Health]] to capture their [[Attributes|Shard]]. Additionally, its Blessing from the Void ability grants +5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Combat Shards.
The deployable lasts for 180s, and resets its duration every time a mob is captured.
## Bugs
- Using a Large Pocket Black Hole on certain mobs may void [[Bestiary]] credit and/or regular item loot.

## Tips
- One of the uses of this item disabling healing is to set your [[Health|HP]] to less than 20% of its maximum, for [[Attributes|Lifeline]] to take effect permanently, which is useful when fighting weaker bosses like [[End Stone Protector]], [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], or [[Kuudra]].
## Trivia
- If the player completes a tier of the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]], they get healed to full HP, even when wearing the Lava Shell Necklace.

The Leaping Sword is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] that can be crafted at [[Spider Eye|Spider Eye IX (9)]]. It can be upgraded into the [[Silk-Edge Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Spider Eye IX (9) ([[Spider Eye]])|1x [[Stick]] 24x [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]] x12 ×2 + [[Stick]] → Leaping Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Leaping Sword [[Damage]]: +150 [[Strength]]: +100 [[Crit Damage]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Leap RIGHT CLICK; Leap into the air and deal 350 damage to nearby enemies upon landing on the ground. Damaged enemies will also be frozen for 1 second.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 50; Cooldown: 1s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 1]
The Leaping Sword's Leap ability allows the player to leap into the air. Upon landing, all enemies within a 4-block radius will receive 350 damage and be stunned for 1s. The ability costs 50 [[Mana]] and has a 1s cooldown.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Silk-Edge Sword]]|None|[[Leaping Sword]] 1x Leaping Sword 1x [[Silky Lichen]] 24x [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]] x6 ×4 + [[Silky Lichen]] + Leaping Sword → [[Silk-Edge Sword]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|20|20|
|✪|5|25|
|✪✪|10|35|
|✪✪✪|15|50|
|✪✪✪✪|20; 10,000 [[Coins]]|70; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|25; 25,000 [[Coins]]|95; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Killing a [[Mob]] using the Leaping Sword's or [[Silk-Edge Sword]]'s ability for the first time awards the [[Player]] with the [[Achievements|"Death From Above" Achievement]].
- Players used to be able to cheese [[Livid]]'s boss fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]] of Dungeons by spamming the Leaping Sword's ability on him to prevent him from cloning himself, allowing the real Livid to be revealed.
  - This method was patched on the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]].
- The Leaping Sword and [[Silk-Edge Sword]] have separate cooldowns, allowing players to use both abilities simultaneously.
- The Leaping Sword references the Spider class in [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]].

The Leatherback Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Hunt Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Leatherback Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Shellwise Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Leatherback Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Hunt Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Hunt Wisdom ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Hunting Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Leatherback Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Leatherback Shard has the following description:

# Lesser Orb of Healing
Aliases: Lesser Orb Of Healing, Orb of Healing, Orb of healing
Categories: Epic items, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Items, Craftable items, Dungeon Items, Power Orbs, Support Items
[Lesser Orb of Healing]
Type: [[Support Items]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon Level ([[Dungeoneering]]) Requirement: Catacombs XVI (16) ([[Catacombs]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 9x [[Mimic Fragment]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LESSER_ORB_OF_HEALING
Tooltip: A shard of a Dungeon Blessing, capable of being bestowed upon another player for a continuous buff, but draining mana.; Orb of Life RIGHT CLICK TEAMMATE Grants +25 Health Regen.; Mana Drain: 15 per second Vitality Drain: 1 per second Max duration: 60s; ❣ Requires Catacombs Skill 16.; EPIC DUNGEON ITEM
A Lesser Orb of Healing is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] item. It can be applied to another [[Player]] to [[Healing|heal]] them.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Mimic Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Mimic Fragment]] ×9 → Lesser Orb of Healing|
## Usage
Item:
Lesser Orb of Healing
[Effects]
[Ability: Orb of Life RIGHT CLICK TEAMMATE; Grants +25 Health Regen.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 15 per second; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 1 per second]
A Lesser Orb of Healing requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XVI (16)]] to use.
When the ability is used, a red orb is sent to a targeted player within 20 blocks. The orb follows and orbits the player indefinitely. The targeted player gains +25 [[Health Regen]]. The player who cast the orb is drained of 15 [[Mana]] and 1 [[Vitality]] per second. Using the ability again recalls the orb. If the caster is depleted of [[Mana]] or [[Vitality]], the orb is automatically recalled.
When the orb is active, the player who is being healed is displayed in the item name, and the item's appearance changes from a red orb to an [[Gray Dye|Enchanted Grey Dye]].
Using its ability does not remove the item, nor does it need to be picked up; the ability spawns a "new" orb.
As it is counted as a Power Orb, its healing effect does not stack with other Power Orbs. However, the [[Mana]] regeneration effect and other buffs from other Orbs will apply.
## Trivia
- The Lesser Orb of Healing is a recolored version of the [[Wizard's Crystal]].
- If used on a player and immediately placed into an item frame, the Lesser Orb of Healing stores the player's name and functions as a glitched kind of [[Cake Soul]].
- [[Ultimate Wise]] reduces the mana drain.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 10, 2026|Changed the buff from 5% [[Health]] of max HP per second while ignoring [[Vitality]] to +25 [[Health Regen]].|
|Jun 10, 2026|Lowered the [[Mana]] cost per second from 50 to 15.|
|Jun 10, 2026|Added a [[Vitality]] cost per second of 1.|
|Jun 10, 2026|Capped the duration at 60s.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Added the Lesser Orb of Healing.|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Fixed the Lesser Orb of Healing not working in [[Dungeons]].|
|Feb 22, 2024|Fixed item requirements only affecting the recipe.|
|Feb 22, 2024|Changed item's tooltip to clarify it not being affected by [[Vitality]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed the buff from 5% [[Health]] of max HP per second while ignoring [[Vitality]] to +25 [[Health Regen]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Lowered the [[Mana]] cost per second from 50 to 15.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added a [[Vitality]] cost per second of 1.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Capped the duration at 60s.|

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Leviathan Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Excellent Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Excellent Karma ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Hunting Fortune]] towards EPIC Attributes.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
