# Lucky Clover
Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Pet items, Pets
[Lucky Clover]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Poignant Lucky Clover]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 40,960x (640 stacks (40,960 items total)) [[Emerald]] 1x [[Lucky Clover Core]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_LUCKY_CLOVER
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +7 Magic Find and +7 Overbloom.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Lucky Clover is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants +7 [[Magic Find]] and +7 [[Overbloom]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Lucky Clover Core]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Emerald]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Emerald]] x32 ×8 + [[Lucky Clover Core]] → Lucky Clover|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Lucky Clover will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +7 [[Magic Find]] and +7 [[Overbloom]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Lucky Clover for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Four-Eyed Fish]]|None|[[Lucky Clover]] 1x Lucky Clover 1x [[Pre-Digestion Fish]] 1x [[Reinforced Scales]] 384x (6 stacks (384 items total)) [[Enchanted Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x64 ×6 + Lucky Clover + [[Pre-Digestion Fish]] + [[Reinforced Scales]] → [[Four-Eyed Fish]]|
### Garden Visitors
The [[Carrot King Avatar]] [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]], summoned by [[Poignant Carrot Bait]], always requests a single Lucky Clover as well as [[Enchanted Golden Carrot|Enchanted Golden Carrots]]. In return, they reward the player with [[Poignant Lucky Clover]].
## Trivia
- The name of this item, Lucky Clover, and its effects, are likely based on Four-leaf clovers, a mutation of a regular clover, commonly believed to give luck if found.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|Buffed [[Overbloom]] from +3 to +7 .|
|Oct 15, 2026|Removed [[Magic Find]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Lucky Clover.|
|May 5, 2026|Lucky Clover now also grants +3 [[Overbloom]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Buffed [[Overbloom]] from +3 to +7 .|

# Lucky Dice
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones
[Lucky Dice]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Lucky
Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Jake]]
Buy: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LUCKY_DICE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Lucky reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Sea Creature Chance  Magic Find; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Lucky Dice is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Lucky]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Sea Creature Chance]] and [[Magic Find]].
## Obtaining
Lucky Dice are obtainable when an [[Oasis Rabbit]] is brought to [[Jake]]. It can then be purchased for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Lucky Dice can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Lucky]] reforge, to grant [[Sea Creature Chance]] and [[Magic Find]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]]. A Fishing Rod that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Lucky]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+2 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+3 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+4 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +3 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+5 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+6 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +7 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+7 MF ([[Magic Find]]) +8 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The texture of the item from 0.27.2 has side with 6 pips, 5 pips and 3 pips, but the 3 pips on the bottom right side were rotated 90°.
- It's the first dice item added into the game, followed by [[Sinful Dice]], [[Archfiend Dice]] and [[High Class Archfiend Dice]].
  - The 3 above have many behaviours in common, that Lucky Dice doesn't.
- Despite what one might expect item called Lucky Dice isn't shown rolling a 6.

The Lunar Moth Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Lunar Power]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Lunar Moth Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Lunar Moth]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Lunar Moth Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Ladybug Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Bug Family
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Lunar Moth Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Lunar Power]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Lunar Power ([[Attributes]]); Grants +5–50 [[Farming Fortune]] during the Night.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Lunar Moth Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Lunar Moth Shard has the following description:
- These beautiful insects are drawn to moonlit areas, where they perform delicate, aerial dances that captivate onlookers.

Aliases: Mantid

[Reforge/Power Stone]

Applied To: [[Armor]]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 75,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MANTID_CLAW
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Mantid reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Bonus Pest Chance  Farming Fortune; Mantid Bonus Grants +0.25 Bonus Pest Chance per  Pest killed for 10 minutes.; Caps at 5 Bonus Pest Chance.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 22.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
A Mantid Claw is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Mantid]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], which grants [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Reforging
Mantid Claw can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Mantid]] reforge, to grant [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and [[Farming Fortune]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXII (22)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Mantid]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Mantid Bonus, which grants 0.25 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] per pest killed in the past 10 minutes up to 5 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] per piece.
